/**
 * OrderRepository singleton managing placement, updates, and realtime synchronization
 * of client orders with Firebase Realtime Database.
 *
 * @author Student Developer
 * @version 1.0
 */
public class OrderRepository {

    /**
     * Listener interface for notifying UI when orders change in Firebase.

    public interface OnOrdersChangeListener {
        /**
         * Fired when Firebase order snapshot updates.
         */
        void onOrdersChanged();

    /**
     * Updates an order's status in Firebase Realtime Database and notifies local listeners.

     * @param orderId   Unique UUID string for the target order.
     * @param newStatus New status string (e.g. OrderRepository.STATUS_ACCEPTED).
     */
    public void updateOrderStatus(String orderId, String newStatus) {
        // Method Implementation
    }
}

1. MainActivity.java

package com.example.work;

import android.content.Intent;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;

import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;

public class MainActivity extends AppCompatActivity {

    private static final int SPLASH_DELAY_MS = 1500;

    @Override
    protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_main);

        new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed(() -> {
            Intent intent = new Intent(MainActivity.this, RoleSelectionActivity.class);
            startActivity(intent);
            finish();
        }, SPLASH_DELAY_MS);
    }
}

2. RoleSelectionActivity.java

package com.example.work;

import android.content.Intent;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.util.Patterns;
import android.widget.RadioButton;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;

import com.google.android.material.button.MaterialButton;
import com.google.android.material.card.MaterialCardView;
import com.google.android.material.textfield.TextInputEditText;
import com.google.android.material.textfield.TextInputLayout;

public class RoleSelectionActivity extends AppCompatActivity {

    private MaterialCardView cardRoleClient;
    private MaterialCardView cardRoleProvider;
    private RadioButton radioClient;
    private RadioButton radioProvider;
    private TextInputLayout tilUserName;
    private TextInputLayout tilUserEmail;
    private TextInputEditText etUserName;
    private TextInputEditText etUserEmail;

    private boolean isClientSelected = true;

    @Override
    protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_role_selection);

        initViews();
        setupRoleToggle();
    }

    private void initViews() {
        cardRoleClient = findViewById(R.id.cardRoleClient);
        cardRoleProvider = findViewById(R.id.cardRoleProvider);
        radioClient = findViewById(R.id.radioClient);
        radioProvider = findViewById(R.id.radioProvider);
        tilUserName = findViewById(R.id.tilUserName);
        tilUserEmail = findViewById(R.id.tilUserEmail);
        etUserName = findViewById(R.id.etUserName);
        etUserEmail = findViewById(R.id.etUserEmail);
        MaterialButton btnContinue = findViewById(R.id.btnContinue);

        btnContinue.setOnClickListener(v -> validateAndProceed());
    }

    private void setupRoleToggle() {
        cardRoleClient.setOnClickListener(v -> selectClientRole());
        cardRoleProvider.setOnClickListener(v -> selectProviderRole());
    }

    private void selectClientRole() {
        isClientSelected = true;
        radioClient.setChecked(true);
        radioProvider.setChecked(false);
        cardRoleClient.setStrokeColor(getColor(R.color.orange_primary));
        cardRoleClient.setStrokeWidth(dpToPx(2));
        cardRoleProvider.setStrokeColor(getColor(R.color.card_stroke));
        cardRoleProvider.setStrokeWidth(dpToPx(1));
    }

    private void selectProviderRole() {
        isClientSelected = false;
        radioClient.setChecked(false);
        radioProvider.setChecked(true);
        cardRoleProvider.setStrokeColor(getColor(R.color.orange_primary));
        cardRoleProvider.setStrokeWidth(dpToPx(2));
        cardRoleClient.setStrokeColor(getColor(R.color.card_stroke));
        cardRoleClient.setStrokeWidth(dpToPx(1));
    }

    private void validateAndProceed() {
        tilUserName.setError(null);
        tilUserEmail.setError(null);

        String name = etUserName.getText() != null ? etUserName.getText().toString().trim() : "";
        String email = etUserEmail.getText() != null ? etUserEmail.getText().toString().trim() : "";

        boolean nameValid = !name.isEmpty();
        boolean emailValid = !email.isEmpty() && Patterns.EMAIL_ADDRESS.matcher(email).matches();

        if (!nameValid) {
            tilUserName.setError(getString(R.string.err_empty_name));
        }

        if (!emailValid) {
            tilUserEmail.setError(getString(R.string.err_invalid_email));
        }

        if (!nameValid || !emailValid) {
            Toast.makeText(this, "ÙŠØ±Ø¬Ù‰ ÙƒØªØ§Ø¨Ø© Ø§Ø³Ù… ÙˆØ¨Ø±ÙŠØ¯ Ø¥Ù„ÙƒØªØ±ÙˆÙ†ÙŠ ØµØ­ÙŠØ­ÙŠÙ† Ù„ØªØ³Ø¬ÙŠÙ„ Ø§Ù„Ø¯Ø®ÙˆÙ„", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            return;
        }

        Intent intent;
        if (isClientSelected) {
            intent = new Intent(RoleSelectionActivity.this, ClientMainActivity.class);
        } else {
            intent = new Intent(RoleSelectionActivity.this, ProviderMainActivity.class);
        }

        intent.putExtra("USER_NAME", name);
        intent.putExtra("USER_EMAIL", email);
        startActivity(intent);
        finish();
    }

    private int dpToPx(int dp) {
        float density = getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density;
        return Math.round(dp * density);
    }
}

3. ClientMainActivity.java

package com.example.work;

import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.res.ColorStateList;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.View;
import android.widget.HorizontalScrollView;
import android.widget.TextView;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;

import com.google.android.material.button.MaterialButton;
import com.google.android.material.card.MaterialCardView;
import com.google.android.material.chip.Chip;
import com.google.android.material.chip.ChipGroup;
import com.google.android.material.floatingactionbutton.FloatingActionButton;

import java.util.List;

public class ClientMainActivity extends AppCompatActivity {

    private ChipGroup chipGroupCategories;
    private HorizontalScrollView hsvCategories;
    private RecyclerView rvServices;
    private RecyclerView rvOrders;
    private TextView tvEmptyState;
    private TextView tvWelcomeHeader;
    private MaterialButton btnTabBrowseServices;
    private MaterialButton btnTabMyOrders;

    private ServiceAdapter serviceAdapter;
    private OrderAdapter orderAdapter;
    private final ServiceRepository repository = ServiceRepository.getInstance();
    private final OrderRepository orderRepository = OrderRepository.getInstance();

    private String selectedCategory = "Ø§Ù„ÙƒÙ„";
    private boolean isMyOrdersTabActive = false;

    private final ServiceRepository.OnDataChangeListener dataChangeListener = () -> runOnUiThread(this::loadCategoryServices);
    private final OrderRepository.OnOrdersChangeListener ordersChangeListener = () -> runOnUiThread(this::loadMyOrders);

    @Override
    protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_client_main);

        repository.init(this);
        orderRepository.init(this);

        initViews();
        setupUserHeader();
        setupRecyclerViews();
        setupListeners();

        repository.addOnDataChangeListener(dataChangeListener);
        orderRepository.addOnOrdersChangeListener(ordersChangeListener);

        loadCategoryServices();
    }

    @Override
    protected void onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy();
        repository.removeOnDataChangeListener(dataChangeListener);
        orderRepository.removeOnOrdersChangeListener(ordersChangeListener);
    }

    private void initViews() {
        tvWelcomeHeader = findViewById(R.id.tvWelcomeHeader);
        chipGroupCategories = findViewById(R.id.chipGroupCategories);
        hsvCategories = findViewById(R.id.hsvCategories);
        rvServices = findViewById(R.id.rvServices);
        rvOrders = findViewById(R.id.rvOrders);
        tvEmptyState = findViewById(R.id.tvEmptyState);
        btnTabBrowseServices = findViewById(R.id.btnTabBrowseServices);
        btnTabMyOrders = findViewById(R.id.btnTabMyOrders);

        MaterialCardView cardOpenSearchScreen = findViewById(R.id.cardOpenSearchScreen);
        MaterialButton btnSwitchRole = findViewById(R.id.btnSwitchRole);
        FloatingActionButton fabSearch = findViewById(R.id.fabSearch);

        btnSwitchRole.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            Intent intent = new Intent(ClientMainActivity.this, RoleSelectionActivity.class);
            intent.setFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP | Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
            startActivity(intent);
            finish();
        });

        View.OnClickListener openSearchListener = v -> {
            Intent intent = new Intent(ClientMainActivity.this, SearchActivity.class);
            startActivity(intent);
        };

        if (cardOpenSearchScreen != null) {
            cardOpenSearchScreen.setOnClickListener(openSearchListener);
        }

        if (fabSearch != null) {
            fabSearch.setOnClickListener(openSearchListener);
        }
    }

    private void setupUserHeader() {
        String userName = getIntent().getStringExtra("USER_NAME");
        if (userName != null && !userName.trim().isEmpty()) {
            tvWelcomeHeader.setText(String.format("Ø£Ù‡Ù„Ø§Ù‹ Ø¨Ùƒ %s ðŸ‘‹", userName.trim()));
        }
    }

    private void setupRecyclerViews() {
        rvServices.setLayoutManager(new LinearLayoutManager(this));
        serviceAdapter = new ServiceAdapter(service -> {
            Intent intent = new Intent(ClientMainActivity.this, ServiceDetailActivity.class);
            intent.putExtra("SERVICE_ITEM", service);
            startActivity(intent);
        });
        rvServices.setAdapter(serviceAdapter);

        rvOrders.setLayoutManager(new LinearLayoutManager(this));
        orderAdapter = new OrderAdapter(false, new OrderAdapter.OnOrderActionListener() {
            @Override
            public void onOrderContactClick(OrderItem order) {
                if (order.getServiceItem() != null && order.getServiceItem().getProviderPhone() != null && !order.getServiceItem().getProviderPhone().trim().isEmpty()) {
                    String phone = order.getServiceItem().getProviderPhone().trim();
                    String digitsOnly = phone.replaceAll("[^0-9]", "");
                    String cleanedPhone = digitsOnly.length() >= 7 ? digitsOnly : phone;

                    try {
                        String url = "https://api.whatsapp.com/send?phone=" + cleanedPhone +
                                "&text=" + Uri.encode("Ù…Ø±Ø­Ø¨Ø§Ù‹ØŒ Ø£ÙˆØ¯ Ø§Ù„Ù…ØªØ§Ø¨Ø¹Ø© Ø¨Ø´Ø£Ù† Ø·Ù„Ø¨ÙŠ Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…ØªÙƒ: " + order.getServiceItem().getTitle());
                        Intent intent = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(url));
                        startActivity(intent);
                    } catch (Exception e) {
                        try {
                            Intent dialIntent = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_DIAL, Uri.parse("tel:" + phone));
                            startActivity(dialIntent);
                        } catch (Exception ex) {
                            Toast.makeText(ClientMainActivity.this, "ØªØ¹Ø°Ø± Ø§Ù„ØªÙˆØ§ØµÙ„ Ù…Ø¹ Ù…Ù‚Ø¯Ù… Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø©", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                        }
                    }
                } else if (order.getServiceItem() != null) {
                    Intent intent = new Intent(ClientMainActivity.this, ServiceDetailActivity.class);
                    intent.putExtra("SERVICE_ITEM", order.getServiceItem());
                    startActivity(intent);
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(ClientMainActivity.this, "ØªÙØ§ØµÙŠÙ„ Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø© ØºÙŠØ± Ù…ØªÙˆÙØ±Ø©", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                }
            }

            @Override
            public void onInAppChatClick(OrderItem order) {
                Intent intent = new Intent(ClientMainActivity.this, ChatActivity.class);
                intent.putExtra("CHAT_ID", order.getOrderId());
                intent.putExtra("RECEIVER_NAME", order.getServiceItem() != null ? order.getServiceItem().getProviderName() : "Ù…Ù‚Ø¯Ù… Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø©");
                intent.putExtra("SERVICE_TITLE", order.getServiceItem() != null ? order.getServiceItem().getTitle() : "Ø·Ù„Ø¨ Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø©");
                intent.putExtra("CURRENT_ROLE", "client");
                intent.putExtra("CURRENT_USER_NAME", order.getClientName() != null ? order.getClientName() : "Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ù…ÙŠÙ„");
                startActivity(intent);
            }

            @Override
            public void onAcceptOrder(OrderItem order) {}

            @Override
            public void onRejectOrder(OrderItem order) {}

            @Override
            public void onCompleteOrder(OrderItem order) {}
        });
        rvOrders.setAdapter(orderAdapter);
    }

    private void setupListeners() {
        btnTabBrowseServices.setOnClickListener(v -> activateBrowseTab());
        btnTabMyOrders.setOnClickListener(v -> activateMyOrdersTab());

        chipGroupCategories.setOnCheckedStateChangeListener((group, checkedIds) -> {
            if (checkedIds.isEmpty()) {
                selectedCategory = "Ø§Ù„ÙƒÙ„";
            } else {
                int checkedId = checkedIds.get(0);
                Chip chip = group.findViewById(checkedId);
                if (chip != null) {
                    selectedCategory = chip.getText().toString();
                } else {
                    selectedCategory = "Ø§Ù„ÙƒÙ„";
                }
            }
            loadCategoryServices();
        });
    }

    private void activateBrowseTab() {
        isMyOrdersTabActive = false;
        btnTabBrowseServices.setBackgroundTintList(ColorStateList.valueOf(getColor(R.color.brand_primary)));
        btnTabBrowseServices.setTextColor(getColor(R.color.white));

        btnTabMyOrders.setBackgroundTintList(ColorStateList.valueOf(getColor(android.R.color.transparent)));
        btnTabMyOrders.setTextColor(getColor(R.color.text_secondary));

        hsvCategories.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
        rvServices.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
        rvOrders.setVisibility(View.GONE);

        loadCategoryServices();
    }

    private void activateMyOrdersTab() {
        isMyOrdersTabActive = true;
        btnTabMyOrders.setBackgroundTintList(ColorStateList.valueOf(getColor(R.color.brand_primary)));
        btnTabMyOrders.setTextColor(getColor(R.color.white));

        btnTabBrowseServices.setBackgroundTintList(ColorStateList.valueOf(getColor(android.R.color.transparent)));
        btnTabBrowseServices.setTextColor(getColor(R.color.text_secondary));

        hsvCategories.setVisibility(View.GONE);
        rvServices.setVisibility(View.GONE);
        rvOrders.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);

        loadMyOrders();
    }

    private void loadCategoryServices() {
        if (isMyOrdersTabActive) return;

        List<ServiceItem> results = repository.searchServices("", -1, selectedCategory);

        if (results.isEmpty()) {
            tvEmptyState.setText(R.string.no_services_found);
            tvEmptyState.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
            rvServices.setVisibility(View.GONE);
        } else {
            tvEmptyState.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            rvServices.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
            serviceAdapter.setServices(results, -1);
        }
    }

    private void loadMyOrders() {
        List<OrderItem> orders = orderRepository.getAllOrders();

        if (orders.isEmpty()) {
            tvEmptyState.setText("Ù„Ù… ØªÙ‚Ù… Ø¨Ø·Ù„Ø¨ Ø£Ùˆ Ø§Ø³ØªØ®Ø¯Ø§Ù… Ø£ÙŠ Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø© Ø¨Ø¹Ø¯. Ø§Ø¨Ø­Ø« Ø¹Ù† Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø© ÙˆØ§Ø·Ù„Ø¨Ù‡Ø§ Ù„ØªØ¸Ù‡Ø± Ù‡Ù†Ø§!");
            tvEmptyState.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
            rvOrders.setVisibility(View.GONE);
        } else {
            tvEmptyState.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            rvOrders.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
            orderAdapter.setOrders(orders);
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void onResume() {
        super.onResume();
        if (isMyOrdersTabActive) {
            loadMyOrders();
        } else {
            loadCategoryServices();
        }
    }
}

package com.example.work;

import android.content.Intent;

import java.util.Locale;

public class ProviderMainActivity extends AppCompatActivity {

    private RecyclerView rvProviderServices;
    private RecyclerView rvIncomingOrders;
    private TextView tvEmptyPosted;
    private TextView tvProviderHeader;
    private TextView tvPostedServicesCount;
    private TextView tvSectionTitle;
    private MaterialButton btnTabMyServices;
    private MaterialButton btnTabIncomingOrders;

    private final OrderRepository orderRepository = OrderRepository.getInstance();

    private final OrderRepository.OnOrdersChangeListener ordersChangeListener = () -> runOnUiThread(this::loadData);

    }

    private void activateServicesTab() {

        btnTabIncomingOrders.setBackgroundTintList(ColorStateList.valueOf(getColor(android.R.color.transparent)));
        btnTabIncomingOrders.setTextColor(getColor(R.color.text_secondary));

        if (tvSectionTitle != null) tvSectionTitle.setText("ÙƒØ§ÙØ© Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø§Øª Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø·Ø±ÙˆØ­Ø© Ø¨Ø§Ù„Ø³ÙˆÙ‚ ðŸŒ");

        rvIncomingOrders.setVisibility(View.GONE);

    }

    private void activateOrdersTab() {
        isOrdersTabActive = true;
        btnTabIncomingOrders.setBackgroundTintList(ColorStateList.valueOf(getColor(R.color.brand_primary)));
        btnTabIncomingOrders.setTextColor(getColor(R.color.white));

        btnTabMyServices.setBackgroundTintList(ColorStateList.valueOf(getColor(android.R.color.transparent)));
        btnTabMyServices.setTextColor(getColor(R.color.text_secondary));

        if (tvSectionTitle != null) tvSectionTitle.setText("Ø§Ù„Ø·Ù„Ø¨Ø§Øª Ø§Ù„ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯Ø© Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø§ØªÙŠ ðŸ“¥");
        rvProviderServices.setVisibility(View.GONE);

    private void setupUserHeader() {
        String userName = getIntent().getStringExtra("USER_NAME");
        if (userName != null && !userName.trim().isEmpty()) {
            tvProviderHeader.setText(String.format("Ù„ÙˆØ­Ø© %s ðŸ› ï¸", userName.trim()));
        }
    }

    private void setupRecyclerViews() {
        rvProviderServices.setLayoutManager(new LinearLayoutManager(this));
        serviceAdapter = new ServiceAdapter(service -> {
            Intent intent = new Intent(ProviderMainActivity.this, ServiceDetailActivity.class);
            intent.putExtra("SERVICE_ITEM", service);
            startActivity(intent);
        });
        rvProviderServices.setAdapter(serviceAdapter);

        rvIncomingOrders.setLayoutManager(new LinearLayoutManager(this));
        orderAdapter = new OrderAdapter(true, new OrderAdapter.OnOrderActionListener() {
            @Override
            public void onOrderContactClick(OrderItem order) {

    private void loadData() {
        List<ServiceItem> services = repository.getAllServices();
        List<OrderItem> orders = orderRepository.getAllOrders();

        int completedOrdersCount = 0;
        for (OrderItem o : orders) {
            if (o.getStatus() != null && o.getStatus().contains("Ù…ÙƒØªÙ…Ù„Ø©")) {
                completedOrdersCount++;
            }
        }

        if (tvPostedServicesCount != null) {
            if (isOrdersTabActive) {
                tvPostedServicesCount.setText(String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%d Ø·Ù„Ø¨Ø§Øª ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯Ø© (%d Ù…ÙƒØªÙ…Ù„Ø©)", orders.size(), completedOrdersCount));
            } else {
                tvPostedServicesCount.setText(String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%d Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø© Ù…ØªØ§Ø­Ø© Ø¨Ø§Ù„Ø³ÙˆÙ‚ | %d Ø·Ù„Ø¨ Ù…ÙƒØªÙ…Ù„", services.size(), completedOrdersCount));
            }
        }

        if (isOrdersTabActive) {
            if (orders.isEmpty()) {
                tvEmptyPosted.setText("Ù„Ø§ ØªÙˆØ¬Ø¯ Ø·Ù„Ø¨Ø§Øª ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯Ø© Ù…Ù† Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ù…Ù„Ø§Ø¡ Ø­ØªÙ‰ Ø§Ù„Ø¢Ù† ðŸ“¥");
                tvEmptyPosted.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                rvIncomingOrders.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            } else {
                tvEmptyPosted.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                rvIncomingOrders.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                orderAdapter.setOrders(orders);
            }
        } else {
            if (services.isEmpty()) {
                tvEmptyPosted.setText(R.string.no_posted_services);
                tvEmptyPosted.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                rvProviderServices.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            } else {
                tvEmptyPosted.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                rvProviderServices.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                serviceAdapter.setServices(services, -1);
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void onResume() {
        super.onResume();
        loadData();
    }

    private void openWhatsAppOrDial(OrderItem order, String phone) {
        if (phone == null || phone.trim().isEmpty()) return;

        String digitsOnly = phone.replaceAll("[^0-9]", "");
        String cleanedPhone = digitsOnly.length() >= 7 ? digitsOnly : phone.trim();

        try {
            String url = "https://api.whatsapp.com/send?phone=" + cleanedPhone +
                    "&text=" + Uri.encode("Ù…Ø±Ø­Ø¨Ø§Ù‹ " + (order.getClientName() != null ? order.getClientName() : "") + "ØŒ Ø£Ù†Ø§ " + (order.getServiceItem() != null ? order.getServiceItem().getProviderName() : "Ù…Ù‚Ø¯Ù… Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø©") + " Ø¨Ø´Ø£Ù† Ø·Ù„Ø¨Ùƒ Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø©: " + (order.getServiceItem() != null ? order.getServiceItem().getTitle() : ""));
            Intent intent = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(url));
            startActivity(intent);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            try {
                Intent dialIntent = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_DIAL, Uri.parse("tel:" + phone.trim()));
                startActivity(dialIntent);
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                Toast.makeText(ProviderMainActivity.this, "ØªØ¹Ø°Ø± ÙØªØ­ Ø§Ù„ÙˆØ§ØªØ³Ø§Ø¨ Ø£Ùˆ Ø§Ù„Ø§ØªØµØ§Ù„ Ø¨Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ù…ÙŠÙ„: " + order.getClientName(), Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            }
        }
    }
}

5. SearchActivity.java

package com.example.work;

import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.inputmethod.InputMethodManager;
import android.widget.LinearLayout;
import android.widget.TextView;

import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;

import com.google.android.material.button.MaterialButton;
import com.google.android.material.chip.Chip;
import com.google.android.material.chip.ChipGroup;
import com.google.android.material.textfield.TextInputEditText;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

public class SearchActivity extends AppCompatActivity {

    private TextInputEditText etSearchQuery;
    private TextInputEditText etTargetPrice;
    private ChipGroup cgSearchHistory;
    private LinearLayout llSearchHistorySection;
    private LinearLayout llSearchResultsHeader;
    private TextView tvResultsHeaderTitle;
    private RecyclerView rvSearchResults;
    private TextView tvSearchEmptyState;

    private ServiceAdapter adapter;
    private final ServiceRepository repository = ServiceRepository.getInstance();

    private final ServiceRepository.OnDataChangeListener dataChangeListener = () -> runOnUiThread(this::executeSearch);

    @Override
    protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_search);

        repository.init(this);
        repository.addOnDataChangeListener(dataChangeListener);

        initViews();
        setupRecyclerView();
        loadSearchHistory();
        autoFocusKeyboard();
    }

    @Override
    protected void onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy();
        repository.removeOnDataChangeListener(dataChangeListener);
    }

    private void initViews() {
        etSearchQuery = findViewById(R.id.etSearchQuery);
        etTargetPrice = findViewById(R.id.etTargetPrice);
        cgSearchHistory = findViewById(R.id.cgSearchHistory);
        llSearchHistorySection = findViewById(R.id.llSearchHistorySection);
        llSearchResultsHeader = findViewById(R.id.llSearchResultsHeader);
        tvResultsHeaderTitle = findViewById(R.id.tvResultsHeaderTitle);
        rvSearchResults = findViewById(R.id.rvSearchResults);
        tvSearchEmptyState = findViewById(R.id.tvSearchEmptyState);

        findViewById(R.id.btnBack).setOnClickListener(v -> finish());

        TextView tvClearHistory = findViewById(R.id.tvClearHistory);
        MaterialButton btnDoSearch = findViewById(R.id.btnDoSearch);

        btnDoSearch.setOnClickListener(v -> executeSearch());

        tvClearHistory.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            SearchHistoryManager.clearHistory(SearchActivity.this);
            loadSearchHistory();
        });
    }

    private void setupRecyclerView() {
        rvSearchResults.setLayoutManager(new LinearLayoutManager(this));
        adapter = new ServiceAdapter(service -> {
            Intent intent = new Intent(SearchActivity.this, ServiceDetailActivity.class);
            intent.putExtra("SERVICE_ITEM", service);
            startActivity(intent);
        });
        rvSearchResults.setAdapter(adapter);
    }

    private void autoFocusKeyboard() {
        etSearchQuery.requestFocus();
        etSearchQuery.postDelayed(() -> {
            InputMethodManager imm = (InputMethodManager) getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE);
            if (imm != null) {
                imm.showSoftInput(etSearchQuery, 0);
            }
        }, 200);
    }

    private void loadSearchHistory() {
        cgSearchHistory.removeAllViews();
        List<String> history = SearchHistoryManager.getSearchHistory(this);

        if (history.isEmpty()) {
            llSearchHistorySection.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            return;
        }

        llSearchHistorySection.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
        for (String item : history) {
            Chip chip = new Chip(this);
            chip.setText(item);
            chip.setClickable(true);
            chip.setFocusable(true);
            chip.setChipBackgroundColorResource(R.color.orange_chip_bg);
            chip.setTextColor(getColor(R.color.orange_primary));

            chip.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (item.contains(" (Ø¨Ø­Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ $")) {
                    String[] parts = item.split(" \\(Ø¨Ø­Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ \\$");
                    etSearchQuery.setText(parts[0].trim());
                    if (parts.length > 1) {
                        String budgetStr = parts[1].replace(")", "").trim();
                        etTargetPrice.setText(budgetStr);
                    }
                } else if (item.startsWith("Ù…ÙŠØ²Ø§Ù†ÙŠØ© Ø¨Ù€ $")) {
                    etSearchQuery.setText("");
                    etTargetPrice.setText(item.replace("Ù…ÙŠØ²Ø§Ù†ÙŠØ© Ø¨Ù€ $", "").trim());
                } else {
                    etSearchQuery.setText(item.trim());
                    etTargetPrice.setText("");
                }
                executeSearch();
            });

            cgSearchHistory.addView(chip);
        }
    }

    private double getTargetPriceValue(String targetPriceStr) {
        if (!targetPriceStr.isEmpty()) {
            try {
                return Double.parseDouble(targetPriceStr);
            } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }

    private void executeSearch() {
        String query = etSearchQuery.getText() != null ? etSearchQuery.getText().toString().trim() : "";
        String targetPriceStr = etTargetPrice.getText() != null ? etTargetPrice.getText().toString().trim() : "";

        double targetPrice = getTargetPriceValue(targetPriceStr);

        if (query.isEmpty() && targetPrice <= 0) {
            tvSearchEmptyState.setText("ÙŠØ±Ø¬Ù‰ ÙƒØªØ§Ø¨Ø© Ø§Ø³Ù… Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø© Ø£Ùˆ Ø§Ù„Ø³Ø¹Ø± Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø·Ù„ÙˆØ¨ Ù„Ù„Ø¨Ø­Ø«");
            tvSearchEmptyState.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
            rvSearchResults.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            llSearchResultsHeader.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            return;
        }

        // Save to search history
        SearchHistoryManager.addSearchQuery(this, query, targetPrice);
        loadSearchHistory();

        // Perform search
        List<ServiceItem> results = repository.searchServices(query, targetPrice, "Ø§Ù„ÙƒÙ„");

        if (results.isEmpty()) {
            tvSearchEmptyState.setText("Ù„Ù… ÙŠØªÙ… Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ø«ÙˆØ± Ø¹Ù„Ù‰ Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø§Øª Ù…Ø·Ø§Ø¨Ù‚Ø© Ù„Ù„Ø¨Ø­Ø« ÙˆØ§Ù„Ù…ÙŠØ²Ø§Ù†ÙŠØ© Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø­Ø¯Ø¯Ø©. Ø¬Ø±Ø¨ Ø§Ù„Ø¨Ø­Ø« Ø¨Ø³Ø¹Ø± Ø£Ùˆ Ø§Ø³Ù… Ù…Ø®ØªÙ„Ù!");
            tvSearchEmptyState.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
            rvSearchResults.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            llSearchResultsHeader.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
            String headerText = String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "Ù†ØªØ§Ø¦Ø¬ Ø§Ù„Ø¨Ø­Ø« Ù„Ù€: %s",
                    query.isEmpty() ? String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "Ù…ÙŠØ²Ø§Ù†ÙŠØ© $%.0f", targetPrice) : query);
            tvResultsHeaderTitle.setText(headerText);
        } else {
            tvSearchEmptyState.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            rvSearchResults.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
            llSearchResultsHeader.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
            String headerText = String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "Ù†ØªØ§Ø¦Ø¬ Ø§Ù„Ø¨Ø­Ø« (%d Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø© Ù…Ø·Ø§Ø¨Ù‚Ø© ÙˆØ³Ø¹Ø± Ù…Ù‚Ø§Ø±Ø¨):", results.size());
            tvResultsHeaderTitle.setText(headerText);
            adapter.setServices(results, targetPrice);
        }

        // Hide keyboard after search execution
        InputMethodManager imm = (InputMethodManager) getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE);
        if (imm != null) {
            imm.hideSoftInputFromWindow(etSearchQuery.getWindowToken(), 0);
        }
    }
}

6. AddServiceActivity.java

package com.example.work;

import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.graphics.Bitmap;
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory;
import android.graphics.Rect;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.util.Base64;
import android.util.Patterns;
import android.view.MotionEvent;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.inputmethod.InputMethodManager;
import android.widget.ArrayAdapter;
import android.widget.AutoCompleteTextView;
import android.widget.EditText;
import android.widget.ImageView;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.activity.result.ActivityResultLauncher;
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts;
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;

import com.google.android.material.button.MaterialButton;
import com.google.android.material.card.MaterialCardView;
import com.google.android.material.textfield.TextInputEditText;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.util.UUID;

public class AddServiceActivity extends AppCompatActivity {

    private TextInputEditText etTitle;
    private AutoCompleteTextView actvCategory;
    private TextInputEditText etPrice;
    private TextInputEditText etDescription;
    private TextInputEditText etProviderName;
    private TextInputEditText etProviderPhone;
    private ImageView ivSelectedServiceImage;
    private View llImagePlaceholder;

    private Uri selectedImageUri = null;

    private final ActivityResultLauncher<String> imagePickerLauncher =
            registerForActivityResult(new ActivityResultContracts.GetContent(), uri -> {
                if (uri != null) {
                    selectedImageUri = uri;
                    ivSelectedServiceImage.setImageURI(uri);
                    llImagePlaceholder.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                    try {
                        getContentResolver().takePersistableUriPermission(uri, Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION);
                    } catch (Exception ignored) {
                    }
                }
            });

    private final String[] categories = new String[]{"Ø¨Ø±Ù…Ø¬Ø©", "ØªØµÙ…ÙŠÙ…", "ØªØ±Ø¬Ù…Ø©", "ØªØ³ÙˆÙŠÙ‚", "ØµÙŠØ§Ù†Ø©"};

    @Override
    protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_add_service);

        ServiceRepository.getInstance().init(this);

        initViews();
        setupCategoryDropdown();
        setupTouchToHideKeyboard();
    }

    private void initViews() {
        etTitle = findViewById(R.id.etTitle);
        actvCategory = findViewById(R.id.actvCategory);
        etPrice = findViewById(R.id.etPrice);
        etDescription = findViewById(R.id.etDescription);
        etProviderName = findViewById(R.id.etProviderName);
        etProviderPhone = findViewById(R.id.etProviderPhone);
        ivSelectedServiceImage = findViewById(R.id.ivSelectedServiceImage);
        llImagePlaceholder = findViewById(R.id.llImagePlaceholder);

        MaterialCardView cardSelectServiceImage = findViewById(R.id.cardSelectServiceImage);
        cardSelectServiceImage.setOnClickListener(v -> imagePickerLauncher.launch("image/*"));

        findViewById(R.id.btnBack).setOnClickListener(v -> finish());

        MaterialButton btnPublish = findViewById(R.id.btnPublish);
        btnPublish.setOnClickListener(v -> publishService());
    }

    private void setupCategoryDropdown() {
        ArrayAdapter<String> adapter = new ArrayAdapter<>(this, android.R.layout.simple_dropdown_item_1line, categories);
        actvCategory.setAdapter(adapter);
    }

    private void setupTouchToHideKeyboard() {
        View root = findViewById(R.id.rootAddServiceLayout);
        View scrollView = findViewById(R.id.scrollViewContainer);

        View.OnClickListener clickListener = v -> hideSoftKeyboard();

        if (root != null) root.setOnClickListener(clickListener);
        if (scrollView != null) scrollView.setOnClickListener(clickListener);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean dispatchTouchEvent(MotionEvent ev) {
        if (ev.getAction() == MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN) {
            View v = getCurrentFocus();
            if (v instanceof EditText) {
                Rect outRect = new Rect();
                v.getGlobalVisibleRect(outRect);
                if (!outRect.contains((int) ev.getRawX(), (int) ev.getRawY())) {
                    v.clearFocus();
                    hideSoftKeyboard();
                }
            }
        }
        return super.dispatchTouchEvent(ev);
    }

    private void hideSoftKeyboard() {
        View view = this.getCurrentFocus();
        if (view != null) {
            InputMethodManager imm = (InputMethodManager) getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE);
            if (imm != null) {
                imm.hideSoftInputFromWindow(view.getWindowToken(), 0);
            }
        }
    }

    private boolean isValidContact(String contact) {
        if (contact == null || contact.trim().isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }
        String trimmed = contact.trim();
        boolean isEmail = Patterns.EMAIL_ADDRESS.matcher(trimmed).matches();
        boolean isPhone = trimmed.replaceAll("[^0-9]", "").length() >= 7;
        return isEmail || isPhone;
    }

    private int getCategorySampleImage(String category) {
        if (category.contains("ØªØµÙ…ÙŠÙ…")) {
            return R.drawable.img_work_design;
        } else if (category.contains("Ø¨Ø±Ù…Ø¬Ø©")) {
            return R.drawable.img_work_code;
        } else if (category.contains("ØªØ³ÙˆÙŠÙ‚")) {
            return R.drawable.img_work_marketing;
        } else if (category.contains("ØªØ±Ø¬Ù…Ø©")) {
            return R.drawable.img_work_translation;
        } else if (category.contains("ØµÙŠØ§Ù†Ø©")) {
            return R.drawable.img_work_maintenance;
        } else {
            return R.drawable.img_work_code;
        }
    }

    private String uriToBase64(Uri uri) {
        if (uri == null) return null;
        try {
            InputStream inputStream = getContentResolver().openInputStream(uri);
            Bitmap bitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeStream(inputStream);
            if (inputStream != null) inputStream.close();
            if (bitmap == null) return null;

            int maxDim = 500;
            int width = bitmap.getWidth();
            int height = bitmap.getHeight();
            if (width > maxDim || height > maxDim) {
                float ratio = Math.min((float) maxDim / width, (float) maxDim / height);
                width = Math.round(width * ratio);
                height = Math.round(height * ratio);
                bitmap = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(bitmap, width, height, true);
            }

            ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
            bitmap.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 70, baos);
            byte[] bytes = baos.toByteArray();
            return Base64.encodeToString(bytes, Base64.DEFAULT);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private void publishService() {
        hideSoftKeyboard();

        String title = etTitle.getText() != null ? etTitle.getText().toString().trim() : "";
        String category = actvCategory.getText() != null ? actvCategory.getText().toString().trim() : "";
        String priceStr = etPrice.getText() != null ? etPrice.getText().toString().trim() : "";
        String description = etDescription.getText() != null ? etDescription.getText().toString().trim() : "";
        String providerName = etProviderName.getText() != null ? etProviderName.getText().toString().trim() : "";
        String providerPhone = etProviderPhone.getText() != null ? etProviderPhone.getText().toString().trim() : "";

        if (title.isEmpty() || category.isEmpty() || priceStr.isEmpty() || description.isEmpty() || providerName.isEmpty() || providerPhone.isEmpty()) {
            Toast.makeText(this, R.string.err_fill_fields, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            return;
        }

        if (!isValidContact(providerPhone)) {
            Toast.makeText(this, R.string.err_invalid_contact, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
            return;
        }

        double price;
        try {
            price = Double.parseDouble(priceStr);
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            Toast.makeText(this, "ÙŠØ±Ø¬Ù‰ ÙƒØªØ§Ø¨Ø© Ø±Ù‚Ù… Ø³Ø¹Ø± ØµØ­ÙŠØ­", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            return;
        }

        int imageResId = getCategorySampleImage(category);
        String imageUriStr = selectedImageUri != null ? selectedImageUri.toString() : null;
        String imageBase64Str = selectedImageUri != null ? uriToBase64(selectedImageUri) : null;

        ServiceItem newService = new ServiceItem(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                title,
                category,
                description,
                price,
                providerName,
                providerPhone,
                5.0f,
                1,
                imageResId,
                imageUriStr,
                imageBase64Str
        );

        ServiceRepository.getInstance().addService(newService);
        Toast.makeText(this, R.string.service_added_success, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
        finish();
    }
}

package com.example.work;

import android.content.Intent;

import androidx.appcompat.app.AlertDialog;
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;

import com.google.android.material.button.MaterialButton;

import java.util.Locale;

public class ServiceDetailActivity extends AppCompatActivity {

    private ImageView ivDetailWorkPhoto;
    private TextView tvDetailCategory;
    private TextView tvDetailRating;
    private TextView tvDetailTitle;
    private TextView tvDetailPrice;
    private TextView tvDetailDescription;
    private TextView tvDetailProviderName;
    private TextView tvDetailProviderPhone;

    private ServiceItem serviceItem;

    @Override

        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_service_detail);

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            serviceItem = getIntent().getSerializableExtra("SERVICE_ITEM", ServiceItem.class);
        } else {
            @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
            ServiceItem item = (ServiceItem) getIntent().getSerializableExtra("SERVICE_ITEM");
            serviceItem = item;
        }

        if (serviceItem == null) {
            Toast.makeText(this, "ØªØ¹Ø°Ø± ØªØ­Ù…ÙŠÙ„ Ø¨ÙŠØ§Ù†Ø§Øª Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø©", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();

            return;

        populateData();
    }

    private void populateData() {
        if (ivDetailWorkPhoto != null) {
            if (serviceItem.getImageBase64() != null && !serviceItem.getImageBase64().isEmpty()) {
                try {
                    byte[] decodedBytes = Base64.decode(serviceItem.getImageBase64(), Base64.DEFAULT);
                    Bitmap bitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(decodedBytes, 0, decodedBytes.length);
                    if (bitmap != null) {
                        ivDetailWorkPhoto.setImageBitmap(bitmap);
                    } else {
                        ivDetailWorkPhoto.setImageResource(R.drawable.img_work_code);
                    }
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    ivDetailWorkPhoto.setImageResource(R.drawable.img_work_code);
                }
            } else if (serviceItem.getImageUri() != null && !serviceItem.getImageUri().isEmpty()) {
                try {
                    ivDetailWorkPhoto.setImageURI(Uri.parse(serviceItem.getImageUri()));
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    ivDetailWorkPhoto.setImageResource(R.drawable.img_work_code);
                }
            } else if (serviceItem.getImageResId() != 0) {
                ivDetailWorkPhoto.setImageResource(serviceItem.getImageResId());
            } else {
                ivDetailWorkPhoto.setImageResource(R.drawable.img_work_code);
            }
        }

        tvDetailCategory.setText(serviceItem.getCategory());
        tvDetailRating.setText(String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%.1f", serviceItem.getRating()));
        tvDetailTitle.setText(serviceItem.getTitle());
        tvDetailPrice.setText(String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "$%.0f", serviceItem.getPrice()));
        tvDetailDescription.setText(serviceItem.getDescription());
        tvDetailProviderName.setText(String.format("Ù…Ù‚Ø¯Ù… Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø©: %s", serviceItem.getProviderName()));
        tvDetailProviderPhone.setText(String.format("ÙˆØ³ÙŠÙ„Ø© Ø§Ù„ØªÙˆØ§ØµÙ„: %s", serviceItem.getProviderPhone()));
    }

    private void openWhatsApp() {

            Toast.makeText(this, "Ø±Ù‚Ù… Ø§Ù„ØªÙˆØ§ØµÙ„ ØºÙŠØ± Ù…ØªÙˆÙØ±", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            return;
        }

        String digitsOnly = phone.replaceAll("[^0-9]", "");
        String cleanedPhone = digitsOnly.length() >= 7 ? digitsOnly : "962791234567";

        try {
            String url = "https://api.whatsapp.com/send?phone=" + cleanedPhone +
                    "&text=" + Uri.encode("Ù…Ø±Ø­Ø¨Ø§Ù‹ØŒ Ø£ÙˆØ¯ Ø§Ù„Ø§Ø³ØªÙØ³Ø§Ø± Ø¹Ù† Ø®Ø¯Ù…ØªÙƒ: " + serviceItem.getTitle());

            Toast.makeText(this, "ØªØ¹Ø°Ø± ÙØªØ­ ØªØ·Ø¨ÙŠÙ‚ Ø§Ù„ÙˆØ§ØªØ³Ø§Ø¨ Ù…Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø±Ø©", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
        }

    private void makePhoneCall() {
        String phone = serviceItem.getProviderPhone();
        if (phone != null && !phone.trim().isEmpty()) {
            Intent intent = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_DIAL);
            intent.setData(Uri.parse("tel:" + phone.trim()));
            startActivity(intent);
        } else {
            Toast.makeText(this, "Ø±Ù‚Ù… Ø§Ù„Ø§ØªØµØ§Ù„ ØºÙŠØ± Ù…ØªÙˆÙØ±", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
        }
    }

    private void openInAppChat() {

        intent.putExtra("CURRENT_USER_NAME", "Ø¹Ù…ÙŠÙ„ Ù…Ù‡ØªÙ…");
        startActivity(intent);
    }

    private void showOrderConfirmation() {

8. ChatActivity.java

package com.example.work;

import android.os.Bundle;
import android.util.Log;
import android.view.View;
import android.widget.TextView;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;

import com.google.android.material.button.MaterialButton;
import com.google.android.material.textfield.TextInputEditText;
import com.google.firebase.database.DataSnapshot;
import com.google.firebase.database.DatabaseError;
import com.google.firebase.database.DatabaseReference;
import com.google.firebase.database.FirebaseDatabase;
import com.google.firebase.database.ValueEventListener;

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.UUID;

public class ChatActivity extends AppCompatActivity {

    private static final String TAG = "ChatActivity";

    private TextView tvChatReceiverName;
    private TextView tvChatServiceTitle;
    private RecyclerView rvChatMessages;
    private TextInputEditText etChatMessageInput;
    private MaterialButton btnSendChatMessage;

    private MessageAdapter messageAdapter;
    private DatabaseReference chatRef;

    private String chatId;
    private String receiverName;
    private String serviceTitle;
    private String currentRole = "client";
    private String currentUserName = "Ø¹Ù…ÙŠÙ„";

    @Override
    protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_chat);

        chatId = getIntent().getStringExtra("CHAT_ID");
        receiverName = getIntent().getStringExtra("RECEIVER_NAME");
        serviceTitle = getIntent().getStringExtra("SERVICE_TITLE");
        currentRole = getIntent().getStringExtra("CURRENT_ROLE");
        currentUserName = getIntent().getStringExtra("CURRENT_USER_NAME");

        if (chatId == null || chatId.trim().isEmpty()) {
            Toast.makeText(this, "ØªØ¹Ø°Ø± ÙØªØ­ Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø­Ø§Ø¯Ø«Ø©ØŒ Ù…Ø¹Ø±Ù Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø­Ø§Ø¯Ø«Ø© ØºÙŠØ± ØµØ­ÙŠØ­", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            finish();
            return;
        }

        if (currentRole == null) currentRole = "client";
        if (currentUserName == null) currentUserName = currentRole.equals("client") ? "Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ù…ÙŠÙ„" : "Ù…Ù‚Ø¯Ù… Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø©";

        initViews();
        setupRecyclerView();
        connectFirebaseChat();
    }

    private void initViews() {
        tvChatReceiverName = findViewById(R.id.tvChatReceiverName);
        tvChatServiceTitle = findViewById(R.id.tvChatServiceTitle);
        rvChatMessages = findViewById(R.id.rvChatMessages);
        etChatMessageInput = findViewById(R.id.etChatMessageInput);
        btnSendChatMessage = findViewById(R.id.btnSendChatMessage);

        findViewById(R.id.btnBack).setOnClickListener(v -> finish());

        if (tvChatReceiverName != null && receiverName != null) {
            tvChatReceiverName.setText(receiverName);
        }
        if (tvChatServiceTitle != null && serviceTitle != null) {
            tvChatServiceTitle.setText("Ø¨Ø´Ø£Ù†: " + serviceTitle);
        }

        btnSendChatMessage.setOnClickListener(v -> sendMessage());
    }

    private void setupRecyclerView() {
        LinearLayoutManager layoutManager = new LinearLayoutManager(this);
        layoutManager.setStackFromEnd(true);
        rvChatMessages.setLayoutManager(layoutManager);

        messageAdapter = new MessageAdapter(currentRole);
        rvChatMessages.setAdapter(messageAdapter);
    }

    private void connectFirebaseChat() {
        FirebaseHelper.ensureAuthenticated(() -> {
            try {
                FirebaseDatabase db = FirebaseHelper.getDatabase();
                if (db != null) {
                    chatRef = db.getReference("chats").child(chatId).child("messages");
                    listenToMessages();
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Chat Firebase error: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }

    private void listenToMessages() {
        if (chatRef == null) return;

        chatRef.addValueEventListener(new ValueEventListener() {
            @Override
            public void onDataChange(@NonNull DataSnapshot snapshot) {
                List<ChatMessage> list = new ArrayList<>();
                for (DataSnapshot child : snapshot.getChildren()) {
                    ChatMessage msg = child.getValue(ChatMessage.class);
                    if (msg != null) {
                        list.add(msg);
                    }
                }

                list.sort((o1, o2) -> Long.compare(o1.getTimestamp(), o2.getTimestamp()));

                runOnUiThread(() -> {
                    messageAdapter.setMessages(list);
                    if (!list.isEmpty()) {
                        rvChatMessages.smoothScrollToPosition(list.size() - 1);
                    }
                });
            }

            @Override
            public void onCancelled(@NonNull DatabaseError error) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Chat cancelled: " + error.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }

    private void sendMessage() {
        if (etChatMessageInput == null) return;

        String text = etChatMessageInput.getText() != null ? etChatMessageInput.getText().toString().trim() : "";
        if (text.isEmpty()) return;

        String msgId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        String timeFormatted = new SimpleDateFormat("hh:mm a", Locale.getDefault()).format(new Date(now));

        ChatMessage message = new ChatMessage(
                msgId,
                chatId,
                currentUserName,
                currentRole,
                text,
                now,
                timeFormatted
        );

        etChatMessageInput.setText("");

        FirebaseHelper.ensureAuthenticated(() -> {
            try {
                if (chatRef == null) {
                    FirebaseDatabase db = FirebaseHelper.getDatabase();
                    if (db != null) {
                        chatRef = db.getReference("chats").child(chatId).child("messages");
                    }
                }
                if (chatRef != null) {
                    chatRef.child(msgId).setValue(message, (error, ref) -> {
                        if (error != null) {
                            Log.e(TAG, "Failed sending message: " + error.getMessage());
                            Toast.makeText(ChatActivity.this, "ÙØ´Ù„ Ø¥Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø§Ù„Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„Ø©: " + error.getMessage(), Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                        } else {
                            Log.d(TAG, "Message sent successfully!");
                        }
                    });
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Send message exception: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }
}

9. ServiceRepository.java

package com.example.work;

import android.content.Context;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;
import android.util.Log;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;

import com.google.firebase.database.DataSnapshot;
import com.google.firebase.database.DatabaseError;
import com.google.firebase.database.DatabaseReference;
import com.google.firebase.database.FirebaseDatabase;
import com.google.firebase.database.ValueEventListener;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public class ServiceRepository {

    public interface OnDataChangeListener {
        void onDataChanged();
    }

    private static final String TAG = "ServiceRepository";

    private static ServiceRepository instance;
    private final Map<String, ServiceItem> servicesMap = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private final List<OnDataChangeListener> listeners = new ArrayList<>();
    private DatabaseReference databaseRef;
    private Context appContext;

    private ServiceRepository() {
    }

    public static synchronized ServiceRepository getInstance() {
        if (instance == null) {
            instance = new ServiceRepository();
        }
        return instance;
    }

    public void init(Context context) {
        if (context != null) {
            this.appContext = context.getApplicationContext();
        }
        FirebaseHelper.ensureAuthenticated(() -> {
            try {
                FirebaseDatabase db = FirebaseHelper.getDatabase();
                if (db != null) {
                    databaseRef = db.getReference("services");
                    attachFirebaseListener();
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Firebase init error: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }

    public void addOnDataChangeListener(OnDataChangeListener listener) {
        if (listener != null && !listeners.contains(listener)) {
            listeners.add(listener);
        }
    }

    public void removeOnDataChangeListener(OnDataChangeListener listener) {
        if (listener != null) {
            listeners.remove(listener);
        }
    }

    private void notifyListeners() {
        for (OnDataChangeListener listener : new ArrayList<>(listeners)) {
            if (listener != null) {
                listener.onDataChanged();
            }
        }
    }

    private void attachFirebaseListener() {
        if (databaseRef == null) return;

        databaseRef.addValueEventListener(new ValueEventListener() {
            @Override
            public void onDataChange(@NonNull DataSnapshot snapshot) {
                Log.d(TAG, "onDataChange: received " + snapshot.getChildrenCount() + " items from Firebase");
                synchronized (ServiceRepository.this) {
                    for (DataSnapshot child : snapshot.getChildren()) {
                        ServiceItem item = child.getValue(ServiceItem.class);
                        if (item != null && item.getId() != null) {
                            servicesMap.put(item.getId(), item);
                        }
                    }
                }
                notifyListeners();
            }

            @Override
            public void onCancelled(@NonNull DatabaseError error) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Firebase cancelled/error: " + error.getMessage() + " (code: " + error.getCode() + ")");
                if (appContext != null) {
                    new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post(() ->
                            Toast.makeText(appContext, "Ø®Ø·Ø£ Ø§Ù„ÙØ§ÙŠØ±Ø¨ÙŠØ³: " + error.getMessage(), Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                    );
                }
            }
        });
    }

    public synchronized List<ServiceItem> getAllServices() {
        return new ArrayList<>(servicesMap.values());
    }

    public void addService(ServiceItem service) {
        if (service == null || service.getId() == null) return;

        // Immediately store in local memory for instant UI display
        synchronized (this) {
            servicesMap.put(service.getId(), service);
        }
        notifyListeners();

        // Push to Firebase Realtime Database
        FirebaseHelper.ensureAuthenticated(() -> {
            try {
                if (databaseRef == null) {
                    FirebaseDatabase db = FirebaseHelper.getDatabase();
                    if (db != null) {
                        databaseRef = db.getReference("services");
                    }
                }
                if (databaseRef != null) {
                    databaseRef.child(service.getId()).setValue(service, (error, ref) -> {
                        if (error != null) {
                            Log.e(TAG, "Firebase setValue failed: " + error.getMessage() + " (code " + error.getCode() + ")");
                            if (appContext != null) {
                                new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post(() ->
                                        Toast.makeText(appContext, "ØªÙ†Ø¨ÙŠÙ‡ Ø§Ù„ÙØ§ÙŠØ±Ø¨ÙŠØ³: " + error.getMessage(), Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                                );
                            }
                        } else {
                            Log.d(TAG, "Firebase setValue succeeded for service: " + service.getTitle());
                            if (appContext != null) {
                                new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post(() ->
                                        Toast.makeText(appContext, "ØªÙ… Ø­ÙØ¸ Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø© Ø¨Ù†Ø¬Ø§Ø­ ÙÙŠ Ø§Ù„Ø³Ø­Ø§Ø¨Ø© â˜ï¸", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                );
                            }
                        }
                    });
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed pushing service to Firebase: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }

    public synchronized List<ServiceItem> searchServices(String query, double targetPrice, String category) {
        List<ServiceItem> filtered = new ArrayList<>();
        String q = query != null ? query.trim().toLowerCase() : "";

        for (ServiceItem item : servicesMap.values()) {
            boolean matchesQuery = q.isEmpty() ||
                    (item.getTitle() != null && item.getTitle().toLowerCase().contains(q)) ||
                    (item.getDescription() != null && item.getDescription().toLowerCase().contains(q)) ||
                    (item.getCategory() != null && item.getCategory().toLowerCase().contains(q));

            boolean matchesCategory = category == null ||
                    category.isEmpty() ||
                    category.equalsIgnoreCase("Ø§Ù„ÙƒÙ„") ||
                    (item.getCategory() != null && item.getCategory().equalsIgnoreCase(category));

            boolean matchesPriceRange;
            if (targetPrice > 0) {
                double diff = Math.abs(item.getPrice() - targetPrice);
                double maxAllowedDiff = Math.max(25.0, targetPrice * 0.40);
                matchesPriceRange = diff <= maxAllowedDiff;
            } else {
                matchesPriceRange = true;
            }

            if (matchesQuery && matchesCategory && matchesPriceRange) {
                filtered.add(item);
            }
        }

        if (targetPrice > 0) {
            filtered.sort((o1, o2) -> {
                double diff1 = Math.abs(o1.getPrice() - targetPrice);
                double diff2 = Math.abs(o2.getPrice() - targetPrice);
                return Double.compare(diff1, diff2);
            });
        }

        return filtered;
    }
}

        void onOrdersChanged();
    }

    public static final String STATUS_PENDING = "ÙÙŠ Ø§Ù†ØªØ¸Ø§Ø± Ù…ÙˆØ§ÙÙ‚Ø© Ù…Ù‚Ø¯Ù… Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø¯Ù…Ø© â³";

    private Context appContext;

    private OrderRepository() {
    }

    public static synchronized OrderRepository getInstance() {
        if (instance == null) {
            instance = new OrderRepository();
        }
        return instance;
    }

    public void init(Context context) {
        if (context != null) {
            this.appContext = context.getApplicationContext();
        }
        FirebaseHelper.ensureAuthenticated(() -> {
            try {
                FirebaseDatabase db = FirebaseHelper.getDatabase();
                if (db != null) {
                    databaseRef = db.getReference("orders");
                    attachFirebaseListener();
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Firebase init error: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }

    public void addOnOrdersChangeListener(OnOrdersChangeListener listener) {
        if (listener != null && !listeners.contains(listener)) {
            listeners.add(listener);
        }
    }

    public void removeOnOrdersChangeListener(OnOrdersChangeListener listener) {
        if (listener != null) {
            listeners.remove(listener);
        }
    }

    private void notifyListeners() {
        for (OnOrdersChangeListener listener : new ArrayList<>(listeners)) {
            if (listener != null) {
                listener.onOrdersChanged();
            }
        }
    }

    private void attachFirebaseListener() {
        if (databaseRef == null) return;

        databaseRef.addValueEventListener(new ValueEventListener() {
            @Override
            public void onDataChange(@NonNull DataSnapshot snapshot) {
                Log.d(TAG, "onDataChange: received " + snapshot.getChildrenCount() + " orders from Firebase");
                synchronized (OrderRepository.this) {
                    for (DataSnapshot child : snapshot.getChildren()) {
                        OrderItem item = child.getValue(OrderItem.class);
                        if (item != null && item.getOrderId() != null) {
                            ordersMap.put(item.getOrderId(), item);
                        }
                    }
                }
                notifyListeners();
            }

            @Override
            public void onCancelled(@NonNull DatabaseError error) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Firebase cancelled/error: " + error.getMessage());
                if (appContext != null) {
                    new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post(() ->
                            Toast.makeText(appContext, "Ø®Ø·Ø£ Ø§Ù„ÙØ§ÙŠØ±Ø¨ÙŠØ³: " + error.getMessage(), Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                    );
                }
            }
        });

    public synchronized List<OrderItem> getAllOrders() {
        return new ArrayList<>(ordersMap.values());
    }

    public void placeOrder(ServiceItem service, String clientName, String clientEmail) {
        placeOrder(service, clientName, clientEmail, null);
    }

    public void placeOrder(ServiceItem service, String clientName, String clientEmail, String clientPhone) {
        String name = clientName != null && !clientName.trim().isEmpty() ? clientName.trim() : "Ø¹Ù…ÙŠÙ„ Ù…Ø³Ø¬Ù„";
        String email = clientEmail != null && !clientEmail.trim().isEmpty() ? clientEmail.trim() : "client@example.com";
        String phone = clientPhone != null && !clientPhone.trim().isEmpty() ? clientPhone.trim() : "";

        OrderItem order = new OrderItem(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                service,
                name,
                email,
                phone,
                getCurrentFormattedDate(),
                STATUS_PENDING
        );

        synchronized (this) {
            ordersMap.put(order.getOrderId(), order);
        }
        notifyListeners();

        FirebaseHelper.ensureAuthenticated(() -> {
            try {
                if (databaseRef == null) {
                    FirebaseDatabase db = FirebaseHelper.getDatabase();
                    if (db != null) {
                        databaseRef = db.getReference("orders");
                    }
                }
                if (databaseRef != null) {
                    databaseRef.child(order.getOrderId()).setValue(order, (error, ref) -> {
                        if (error != null) {
                            Log.e(TAG, "Firebase setValue order failed: " + error.getMessage());
                            if (appContext != null) {
                                new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post(() ->
                                        Toast.makeText(appContext, "ØªÙ†Ø¨ÙŠÙ‡ Ø§Ù„ÙØ§ÙŠØ±Ø¨ÙŠØ³: " + error.getMessage(), Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                                );
                            }
                        } else {
                            Log.d(TAG, "Firebase setValue order succeeded!");
                        }
                    });
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed pushing order to Firebase: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }

    public void updateOrderStatus(String orderId, String newStatus) {
        if (orderId == null || newStatus == null) return;

        synchronized (this) {
            OrderItem order = ordersMap.get(orderId);
            if (order != null) {
                order.setStatus(newStatus);
            }
        }
        notifyListeners();

        FirebaseHelper.ensureAuthenticated(() -> {
            try {
                if (databaseRef == null) {
                    FirebaseDatabase db = FirebaseHelper.getDatabase();
                    if (db != null) {
                        databaseRef = db.getReference("orders");
                    }
                }
                if (databaseRef != null) {
                    databaseRef.child(orderId).child("status").setValue(newStatus, (error, ref) -> {
                        if (error != null) {
                            Log.e(TAG, "Firebase updateOrderStatus failed: " + error.getMessage());
                            if (appContext != null) {
                                new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post(() ->
                                        Toast.makeText(appContext, "ØªÙ†Ø¨ÙŠÙ‡ Ø§Ù„ÙØ§ÙŠØ±Ø¨ÙŠØ³: " + error.getMessage(), Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                                );
                            }
                        } else {
                            Log.d(TAG, "Firebase updateOrderStatus succeeded to: " + newStatus);
                        }
                    });
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed updating order status in Firebase: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }

    private String getCurrentFormattedDate() {
        SimpleDateFormat sdf = new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", Locale.getDefault());
        return sdf.format(new Date());
    }
}

11. FirebaseHelper.java

package com.example.work;

import android.util.Log;

import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth;
import com.google.firebase.database.FirebaseDatabase;

public class FirebaseHelper {
    private static final String TAG = "FirebaseHelper";
    private static final String DB_URL_US = "https://work-4451a-default-rtdb.firebaseio.com";
    private static final String DB_URL_EU = "https://work-4451a-default-rtdb.europe-west1.firebasedatabase.app";
    private static final String DB_URL_ASIA = "https://work-4451a-default-rtdb.asia-southeast1.firebasedatabase.app";
    private static final String DB_URL_ALT = "https://work-4451a.firebaseio.com";

    private static FirebaseDatabase databaseInstance;

    public interface OnAuthCompleteListener {
        void onAuthSuccess();
    }

    public static synchronized FirebaseDatabase getDatabase() {
        if (databaseInstance == null) {
            String[] urls = new String[]{DB_URL_US, DB_URL_EU, DB_URL_ASIA, DB_URL_ALT};
            for (String url : urls) {
                try {
                    FirebaseDatabase db = FirebaseDatabase.getInstance(url);
                    if (db != null) {
                        databaseInstance = db;
                        break;
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {
                }
            }

            if (databaseInstance == null) {
                try {
                    databaseInstance = FirebaseDatabase.getInstance();
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    Log.e(TAG, "Error getting default FirebaseDatabase instance: " + e.getMessage());
                }
            }

            if (databaseInstance != null) {
                try {
                    databaseInstance.setPersistenceEnabled(true);
                } catch (Exception ignored) {
                }
            }
        }
        return databaseInstance;
    }

    public static void ensureAuthenticated(OnAuthCompleteListener listener) {
        try {
            FirebaseAuth auth = FirebaseAuth.getInstance();
            if (auth.getCurrentUser() != null) {
                if (listener != null) listener.onAuthSuccess();
                return;
            }

            auth.signInAnonymously().addOnCompleteListener(task -> {
                if (task.isSuccessful()) {
                    Log.d(TAG, "Signed in anonymously to Firebase Auth successfully!");
                } else {
                    Log.e(TAG, "Anonymous sign in failed: " + (task.getException() != null ? task.getException().getMessage() : "Unknown"));
                }
                if (listener != null) listener.onAuthSuccess();
            });
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Auth error: " + e.getMessage());
            if (listener != null) listener.onAuthSuccess();
        }
    }
}

12. SearchHistoryManager.java

package com.example.work;

import android.content.Context;
import android.content.SharedPreferences;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;

public class SearchHistoryManager {

    private static final String PREF_NAME = "work_search_history";
    private static final String KEY_HISTORY = "history_items";

    public static void addSearchQuery(Context context, String query, double budget) {
        String entry;
        String trimmed = query != null ? query.trim() : "";
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) {
            if (budget <= 0) return;
            entry = "Ù…ÙŠØ²Ø§Ù†ÙŠØ© Ø¨Ù€ $" + ((int) budget);
        } else if (budget > 0) {
            entry = trimmed + " (Ø¨Ø­Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ $" + ((int) budget) + ")";
        } else {
            entry = trimmed;
        }

        SharedPreferences prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
        Set<String> set = prefs.getStringSet(KEY_HISTORY, new HashSet<>());
        Set<String> newSet = new HashSet<>(set);
        newSet.add(entry);

        prefs.edit().putStringSet(KEY_HISTORY, newSet).apply();
    }

    public static List<String> getSearchHistory(Context context) {
        SharedPreferences prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
        Set<String> set = prefs.getStringSet(KEY_HISTORY, new HashSet<>());
        return new ArrayList<>(set);
    }

    public static void clearHistory(Context context) {
        SharedPreferences prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
        prefs.edit().remove(KEY_HISTORY).apply();
    }
}

        this.imageBase64 = imageBase64;
    }

    public ServiceItem(String id, String title, String category, String description, double price, String providerName, String providerPhone, float rating, int reviewsCount, int imageResId, String imageUri) {
        this(id, title, category, description, price, providerName, providerPhone, rating, reviewsCount, imageResId, imageUri, null);

        this(id, title, category, description, price, providerName, providerPhone, rating, reviewsCount, imageResId, null, null);
    }

    public ServiceItem(String id, String title, String category, String description, double price, String providerName, String providerPhone, float rating, int reviewsCount) {

            json.put("id", id);
            json.put("title", title);
            json.put("category", category);
            json.put("description", description);
            json.put("price", price);
            json.put("providerName", providerName);

            json.put("rating", rating);

            if (imageBase64 != null) json.put("imageBase64", imageBase64);

            e.printStackTrace();

        return json;
    }

    public static ServiceItem fromJsonObject(JSONObject json) {

                json.optString("description", ""),

                json.optString("providerName", ""),

                (float) json.optDouble("rating", 5.0),
                json.optInt("reviewsCount", 1),
                json.optInt("imageResId", R.drawable.img_work_code),
                json.has("imageUri") ? json.optString("imageUri", null) : null,
                json.has("imageBase64") ? json.optString("imageBase64", null) : null
        );
    }

package com.example.work;

import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.io.Serializable;

public class OrderItem implements Serializable {
    private String orderId;
    private ServiceItem serviceItem;
    private String clientName;
    private String clientEmail;
    private String clientPhone;
    private String orderDate;
    private String status;

    public OrderItem() {
    }

    public OrderItem(String orderId, ServiceItem serviceItem, String clientName, String clientEmail, String clientPhone, String orderDate, String status) {
        this.orderId = orderId;
        this.serviceItem = serviceItem;
        this.clientName = clientName;
        this.clientEmail = clientEmail;
        this.clientPhone = clientPhone;
        this.orderDate = orderDate;
        this.status = status;
    }

    public OrderItem(String orderId, ServiceItem serviceItem, String clientName, String clientEmail, String orderDate, String status) {
        this(orderId, serviceItem, clientName, clientEmail, null, orderDate, status);
    }

    public JSONObject toJsonObject() {
        JSONObject json = new JSONObject();
        try {
            json.put("orderId", orderId);
            if (serviceItem != null) {
                json.put("serviceItem", serviceItem.toJsonObject());
            }
            json.put("clientName", clientName);
            json.put("clientEmail", clientEmail);
            json.put("clientPhone", clientPhone);
            json.put("orderDate", orderDate);
            json.put("status", status);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
        return json;
    }

    public static OrderItem fromJsonObject(JSONObject json) {
        if (json == null) return null;
        ServiceItem service = null;
        if (json.has("serviceItem")) {
            service = ServiceItem.fromJsonObject(json.optJSONObject("serviceItem"));
        }
        return new OrderItem(
                json.optString("orderId", ""),
                service,
                json.optString("clientName", ""),
                json.optString("clientEmail", ""),
                json.optString("clientPhone", ""),
                json.optString("orderDate", ""),
                json.optString("status", "")
        );
    }

    public String getOrderId() {
        return orderId;
    }

    public void setOrderId(String orderId) {
        this.orderId = orderId;
    }

    public ServiceItem getServiceItem() {
        return serviceItem;
    }

    public void setServiceItem(ServiceItem serviceItem) {
        this.serviceItem = serviceItem;
    }

    public String getClientName() {
        return clientName;
    }

    public void setClientName(String clientName) {
        this.clientName = clientName;
    }

    public String getClientEmail() {
        return clientEmail;
    }

    public void setClientEmail(String clientEmail) {
        this.clientEmail = clientEmail;
    }

    public String getClientPhone() {
        return clientPhone;
    }

    public void setClientPhone(String clientPhone) {
        this.clientPhone = clientPhone;
    }

    public String getOrderDate() {
        return orderDate;
    }

    public void setOrderDate(String orderDate) {
        this.orderDate = orderDate;
    }

    public String getStatus() {
        return status;
    }

    public void setStatus(String status) {
        this.status = status;
    }
}
