16. ServiceAdapter.java

package com.example.work;

import android.graphics.Bitmap;
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.util.Base64;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.ImageView;
import android.widget.TextView;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

public class ServiceAdapter extends RecyclerView.Adapter<ServiceAdapter.ServiceViewHolder> {

    public interface OnServiceClickListener {
        void onServiceClick(ServiceItem service);
    }

    private List<ServiceItem> services = new ArrayList<>();
    private final OnServiceClickListener listener;
    private double currentTargetPrice = -1;

    public ServiceAdapter(OnServiceClickListener listener) {
        this.listener = listener;
    }

    public void setServices(List<ServiceItem> newServices, double targetPrice) {
        this.services = newServices != null ? newServices : new ArrayList<>();
        this.currentTargetPrice = targetPrice;
        notifyDataSetChanged();
    }

    @NonNull
    @Override
    public ServiceViewHolder onCreateViewHolder(@NonNull ViewGroup parent, int viewType) {
        View view = LayoutInflater.from(parent.getContext()).inflate(R.layout.item_service, parent, false);
        return new ServiceViewHolder(view);
    }

    @Override
    public void onBindViewHolder(@NonNull ServiceViewHolder holder, int position) {
        ServiceItem item = services.get(position);
        holder.bind(item, currentTargetPrice, listener);
    }

    @Override
    public int getItemCount() {
        return services.size();
    }

    static class ServiceViewHolder extends RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
        private final ImageView ivServiceThumbnail;
        private final TextView tvCategoryBadge;
        private final TextView tvRating;
        private final TextView tvReviewsCount;
        private final TextView tvServiceTitle;
        private final TextView tvProviderName;
        private final TextView tvPriceValue;
        private final TextView tvPriceSimilarityBadge;

        public ServiceViewHolder(@NonNull View itemView) {
            super(itemView);
            ivServiceThumbnail = itemView.findViewById(R.id.ivServiceThumbnail);
            tvCategoryBadge = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvCategoryBadge);
            tvRating = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvRating);
            tvReviewsCount = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvReviewsCount);
            tvServiceTitle = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvServiceTitle);
            tvProviderName = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvProviderName);
            tvPriceValue = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvPriceValue);
            tvPriceSimilarityBadge = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvPriceSimilarityBadge);
        }

        public void bind(ServiceItem item, double targetPrice, OnServiceClickListener listener) {
            if (ivServiceThumbnail != null) {
                if (item.getImageBase64() != null && !item.getImageBase64().isEmpty()) {
                    try {
                        byte[] decodedBytes = Base64.decode(item.getImageBase64(), Base64.DEFAULT);
                        Bitmap bitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(decodedBytes, 0, decodedBytes.length);
                        if (bitmap != null) {
                            ivServiceThumbnail.setImageBitmap(bitmap);
                        } else {
                            ivServiceThumbnail.setImageResource(R.drawable.img_work_code);
                        }
                    } catch (Exception e) {
                        ivServiceThumbnail.setImageResource(R.drawable.img_work_code);
                    }
                } else if (item.getImageUri() != null && !item.getImageUri().isEmpty()) {
                    try {
                        ivServiceThumbnail.setImageURI(Uri.parse(item.getImageUri()));
                    } catch (Exception e) {
                        ivServiceThumbnail.setImageResource(R.drawable.img_work_code);
                    }
                } else if (item.getImageResId() != 0) {
                    ivServiceThumbnail.setImageResource(item.getImageResId());
                } else {
                    ivServiceThumbnail.setImageResource(R.drawable.img_work_code);
                }
            }

            tvCategoryBadge.setText(item.getCategory());
            tvRating.setText(String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%.1f", item.getRating()));
            tvReviewsCount.setText(String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "(%d)", item.getReviewsCount()));
            tvServiceTitle.setText(item.getTitle());
            tvProviderName.setText(String.format("Ø¨ÙˆØ§Ø³Ø·Ø©: %s", item.getProviderName()));
            tvPriceValue.setText(String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%.0f", item.getPrice()));

            // Display price similarity indicator if user specified a budget
            if (targetPrice > 0) {
                tvPriceSimilarityBadge.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                double diff = item.getPrice() - targetPrice;
                if (Math.abs(diff) < 0.01) {
                    tvPriceSimilarityBadge.setText("Ø³Ø¹Ø± Ù…Ø·Ø§Ø¨Ù‚ Ù„Ù…ÙŠØ²Ø§Ù†ÙŠØªÙƒ Ø¨Ø§Ù„Ø¶Ø¨Ø·! ðŸŽ¯");
                } else if (diff < 0) {
                    tvPriceSimilarityBadge.setText(String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "Ø£Ù‚Ù„ Ù…Ù† Ù…ÙŠØ²Ø§Ù†ÙŠØªÙƒ Ø¨Ù€ $%.0f ðŸ’¡", Math.abs(diff)));
                } else {
                    tvPriceSimilarityBadge.setText(String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "Ø£Ø¹Ù„Ù‰ Ù…Ù† Ù…ÙŠØ²Ø§Ù†ÙŠØªÙƒ Ø¨Ù€ $%.0f", diff));
                }
            } else {
                tvPriceSimilarityBadge.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            }

            itemView.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) {
                    listener.onServiceClick(item);
                }
            });
        }
    }
}

17. OrderAdapter.java

package com.example.work;

import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.TextView;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;

import com.google.android.material.button.MaterialButton;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

public class OrderAdapter extends RecyclerView.Adapter<OrderAdapter.OrderViewHolder> {

    public interface OnOrderActionListener {
        void onOrderContactClick(OrderItem order);
        void onInAppChatClick(OrderItem order);
        void onAcceptOrder(OrderItem order);
        void onRejectOrder(OrderItem order);
        void onCompleteOrder(OrderItem order);
    }

    private List<OrderItem> orders = new ArrayList<>();
    private final OnOrderActionListener listener;
    private boolean isProviderMode = false;

    public OrderAdapter(OnOrderActionListener listener) {
        this.listener = listener;
    }

    public OrderAdapter(boolean isProviderMode, OnOrderActionListener listener) {
        this.isProviderMode = isProviderMode;
        this.listener = listener;
    }

    public void setOrders(List<OrderItem> newOrders) {
        this.orders = newOrders != null ? newOrders : new ArrayList<>();
        notifyDataSetChanged();
    }

    @NonNull
    @Override
    public OrderViewHolder onCreateViewHolder(@NonNull ViewGroup parent, int viewType) {
        View view = LayoutInflater.from(parent.getContext()).inflate(R.layout.item_order, parent, false);
        return new OrderViewHolder(view);
    }

    @Override
    public void onBindViewHolder(@NonNull OrderViewHolder holder, int position) {
        OrderItem item = orders.get(position);
        holder.bind(item, isProviderMode, listener);
    }

    @Override
    public int getItemCount() {
        return orders.size();
    }

    static class OrderViewHolder extends RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
        private final TextView tvOrderStatus;
        private final TextView tvOrderDate;
        private final TextView tvOrderServiceTitle;
        private final TextView tvOrderProviderName;
        private final TextView tvOrderClientName;
        private final TextView tvOrderPrice;
        private final View llProviderActions;
        private final MaterialButton btnAcceptOrder;
        private final MaterialButton btnRejectOrder;
        private final MaterialButton btnCompleteOrder;
        private final MaterialButton btnInAppChat;
        private final MaterialButton btnOrderContactProvider;

        public OrderViewHolder(@NonNull View itemView) {
            super(itemView);
            tvOrderStatus = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvOrderStatus);
            tvOrderDate = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvOrderDate);
            tvOrderServiceTitle = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvOrderServiceTitle);
            tvOrderProviderName = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvOrderProviderName);
            tvOrderClientName = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvOrderClientName);
            tvOrderPrice = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvOrderPrice);
            llProviderActions = itemView.findViewById(R.id.llProviderActions);
            btnAcceptOrder = itemView.findViewById(R.id.btnAcceptOrder);
            btnRejectOrder = itemView.findViewById(R.id.btnRejectOrder);
            btnCompleteOrder = itemView.findViewById(R.id.btnCompleteOrder);
            btnInAppChat = itemView.findViewById(R.id.btnInAppChat);
            btnOrderContactProvider = itemView.findViewById(R.id.btnOrderContactProvider);
        }

        public void bind(OrderItem order, boolean isProviderMode, OnOrderActionListener listener) {
            ServiceItem service = order.getServiceItem();

            tvOrderStatus.setText(order.getStatus() != null ? order.getStatus() : OrderRepository.STATUS_PENDING);
            tvOrderDate.setText(String.format("ØªØ§Ø±ÙŠØ® Ø§Ù„Ø·Ù„Ø¨: %s", order.getOrderDate()));

            if (service != null) {
                tvOrderServiceTitle.setText(service.getTitle());
                tvOrderProviderName.setText(String.format("Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø²ÙˆØ¯: %s", service.getProviderName()));
                tvOrderPrice.setText(String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "$%.0f", service.getPrice()));
            }

            if (isProviderMode) {
                if (tvOrderClientName != null) {
                    tvOrderClientName.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                    String clientInfo = String.format("Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ù…ÙŠÙ„ Ø§Ù„Ø·Ø§Ù„Ø¨: %s (%s)",
                            order.getClientName() != null ? order.getClientName() : "Ø¹Ù…ÙŠÙ„",
                            order.getClientEmail() != null ? order.getClientEmail() : "");
                    tvOrderClientName.setText(clientInfo);
                }

                String status = order.getStatus();
                if (status == null || status.contains("ÙÙŠ Ø§Ù†ØªØ¸Ø§Ø±")) {
                    llProviderActions.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                    btnCompleteOrder.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                } else if (status.contains("ØªÙ… Ù‚Ø¨ÙˆÙ„") || status.contains("Ù‚ÙŠØ¯ Ø§Ù„ØªÙ†ÙÙŠØ°")) {
                    llProviderActions.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                    btnCompleteOrder.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                } else {
                    llProviderActions.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                    btnCompleteOrder.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                }
            } else {
                if (tvOrderClientName != null) {
                    tvOrderClientName.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                }
                llProviderActions.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                btnCompleteOrder.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            }

            btnAcceptOrder.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) listener.onAcceptOrder(order);
            });

            btnRejectOrder.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) listener.onRejectOrder(order);
            });

            btnCompleteOrder.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) listener.onCompleteOrder(order);
            });

            btnOrderContactProvider.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) listener.onOrderContactClick(order);
            });

            btnInAppChat.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) listener.onInAppChatClick(order);
            });
        }
    }
}

18. MessageAdapter.java

package com.example.work;

import android.content.res.ColorStateList;
import android.graphics.Color;
import android.view.Gravity;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.LinearLayout;
import android.widget.TextView;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;

import com.google.android.material.card.MaterialCardView;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class MessageAdapter extends RecyclerView.Adapter<MessageAdapter.MessageViewHolder> {

    private List<ChatMessage> messages = new ArrayList<>();
    private String currentUserRole = "client";

    public MessageAdapter(String currentUserRole) {
        this.currentUserRole = currentUserRole != null ? currentUserRole : "client";
    }

    public void setMessages(List<ChatMessage> newMessages) {
        this.messages = newMessages != null ? newMessages : new ArrayList<>();
        notifyDataSetChanged();
    }

    @NonNull
    @Override
    public MessageViewHolder onCreateViewHolder(@NonNull ViewGroup parent, int viewType) {
        View view = LayoutInflater.from(parent.getContext()).inflate(R.layout.item_chat_message, parent, false);
        return new MessageViewHolder(view);
    }

    @Override
    public void onBindViewHolder(@NonNull MessageViewHolder holder, int position) {
        ChatMessage message = messages.get(position);
        holder.bind(message, currentUserRole);
    }

    @Override
    public int getItemCount() {
        return messages.size();
    }

    static class MessageViewHolder extends RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
        private final LinearLayout llMessageContainer;
        private final MaterialCardView cardMessageBubble;
        private final TextView tvMessageSender;
        private final TextView tvMessageText;
        private final TextView tvMessageTime;

        public MessageViewHolder(@NonNull View itemView) {
            super(itemView);
            llMessageContainer = itemView.findViewById(R.id.llMessageContainer);
            cardMessageBubble = itemView.findViewById(R.id.cardMessageBubble);
            tvMessageSender = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvMessageSender);
            tvMessageText = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvMessageText);
            tvMessageTime = itemView.findViewById(R.id.tvMessageTime);
        }

        public void bind(ChatMessage message, String currentUserRole) {
            boolean isMyMessage = currentUserRole.equalsIgnoreCase(message.getSenderRole());

            tvMessageSender.setText(message.getSenderName() != null ? message.getSenderName() : "");
            tvMessageText.setText(message.getText() != null ? message.getText() : "");
            tvMessageTime.setText(message.getTimeFormatted() != null ? message.getTimeFormatted() : "");

            if (isMyMessage) {
                llMessageContainer.setGravity(Gravity.END);
                cardMessageBubble.setCardBackgroundColor(ColorStateList.valueOf(ContextCompat.getColor(itemView.getContext(), R.color.orange_primary)));
                tvMessageSender.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
                tvMessageText.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
                tvMessageTime.setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#FEF08A"));
            } else {
                llMessageContainer.setGravity(Gravity.START);
                cardMessageBubble.setCardBackgroundColor(ColorStateList.valueOf(ContextCompat.getColor(itemView.getContext(), R.color.surface_white)));
                tvMessageSender.setTextColor(ContextCompat.getColor(itemView.getContext(), R.color.orange_primary));
                tvMessageText.setTextColor(ContextCompat.getColor(itemView.getContext(), R.color.text_primary));
                tvMessageTime.setTextColor(ContextCompat.getColor(itemView.getContext(), R.color.text_secondary));
            }
        }
    }
}
